torch_dtype=torch_dtype)\\n        ref_model.eval()\\n        for p in ref_model.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        return ref_model\\n    return _original_create_model(model_id, architecture=architecture, **kwargs)\\n\\n\\n_grpo_module.create_model_from_path = _create_pruned_ref_model\\n# --- End monkey-patch ---\\n\\n\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\n    random.seed(seed)\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardDesertCallback(TrainerCallback):\\n    def __init__(self, patience: int = 50):\\n        self.patience = patience\\n        self.zero_reward_count = 0\\n        self.last_logged_step = -1\\n\\n    def on_log(self, args, state, control, logs=None, **kwargs):\\n        if logs is None:\\n            return\\n        reward_mean = logs.get(\\\"reward\\\", logs.get(\\\"rewards/mean\\\", None))\\n        if reward_mean is None:\\n            return\\n        if state.global_step == self.last_logged_step:\\n            return\\n        self.last_logged_step = state.global_step\\n\\n        if reward_mean == 0.0:\\n            self.zero_reward_count += 1\\n            if self.zero_reward_count >= self.patience:\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    f\\\"REWARD DESERT: reward=0 for {self.zero_reward_count}   return str(val)\\n    except (ValueError, OverflowError):\\n        return ans\\n\\n\\ndef extract_gold_answer(answer_text: str) -> str:\\n    match = re.search(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", prepare_dataset(tokenizer, max_samples: int = None, seed: int = 42):\\n    ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"train\\\")\\n    if max_samples is not None:\\n        ds = gold_answers = kwargs.get(\\\"answer\\\", None)\\n        rewards = []\\n        for i, completion in enumerate(completions):\\n            if isinstance(completion, list):\\n                text = completion[-1][\\\"content\\\"] if completion else \\\"\\\"\\n            else:\\n                text = completion\\n\\n            pred = extract_answer(text)\\n\\n            if gold_answers is not None and i < len(gold_answers):\\n                gold = normalize_answer(str(gold_answers[i]))\\n            else:\\n                gold = None\\n\\n            if pred is not None and gold is not None:\\n                pred_norm = normalize_answer(pred)\\n                accuracy_reward = 1.0 if pred_norm == gold else 0.0\\n            else:\\n                accuracy_reward = 0.0\\n\\n            fmt_reward = format_reward_weight if has_boxed_format(text) else 0.0\\n            rewards.append(accuracy_reward + fmt_reward)\\n\\n        return rewards\\n\\n    return reward_fn\\n\\n\\ndef prepare_dataset(tokenizer, max_samples: int = None, seed: int = 42):\\n    ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"train\\\")\\n    if max_samples is not None:\\n        ds = ds.shuffle(seed=seed).select(range(min(max_samples, len(ds))))\\n\\n    def format_prompt(example):\\n        prompt = (\\n            f\\\"Solve the following math problem step by step. \\\"\\n            f\\\"Put your final answer in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"Question: {example['question']}\\\\n\\\\nAnswer:\\\"\\n        )\\n        example[\\\"prompt\\\"] = prompt\\n        example[\\\"answer\\\"] = extract_gold_answer(example[\\\"answer\\\"])\\n        return example\\n\\n    ds = ds.map(format_prompt)\\n    return ds\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, Actually, GRPOConfig extends TrainingArguments in TRL, which definitely has the `optim` field. The value "adamw_8bit" tells the HuggingFace Trainer to use bitsandbytes AdamW8bit. This should work.


```text
Now I'm moving to Phase 4 and need to submit the training jobs. Since there's no direct submission tool, I'll use SSH to launch them in tmux sessions and track everything through the registry. Let me set up the training commands with the specific parameters for RASP v7: the experiment ID, server location, GPU assignment, model path, and all the hyperparameters like completion length, beta, temperature, and repetition penalty.

For the Standard v7 variant, I'll use the same approach but with its own model path and GPU. Both runs need proper CUDA device restrictions, output directories on the data disk, wandb tracking with the experiment IDs as run names, and file logging. I should verify the model paths exist first, then construct the tmux commands to launch both trainings in parallel.
```

```text
They're actively computing. The log not updating is likely because Python's stdout is buffered and `tee` hasn't flushed yet. This is the model loading/dataset prep phase which is CPU-bound (no GPU mem used yet).

The models are about ~11 GB each in bf16, and they're loading into CPU RAM first before being moved to GPU. 

With 15 GB RSS, they're well into the loading process and this is completely normal—the log will update once the buffer flushes or the next logging call happens. Both processes are healthy and just working through the model loading phase, which takes a few minutes for models this size.
```

</details>
